import { ENV } from './config/env';
import { MULTI_CHAIN_ARBITRAGE_PAIRS, SWARM_AGENTS } from './config/registry';
import { SUPPORTED_CHAINS } from './config/chains';
import { RPCManager } from './providers/rpcManager';
import { SwarmManager } from './cluster/swarmManager';
import { startServer, setSwarmManager } from './server';
import { logger } from './utils/logger';
import { telegramNotifier } from './notifications/telegramNotifier';
import { loadPersistedStats } from './utils/statsStore';
import { Permit2ApprovalManager } from './executor/permit2ApprovalManager';
import { SweetSpotOptimizer } from './math/sweetSpotOptimizer';
import { ArbitrumSequencerFeedListener, BaseFlashblockFeedListener } from './mempool/mempoolListener';

async function main() {
  // Cargar estadísticas históricas desde disco
  loadPersistedStats();

  logger.divider();
  logger.info('🚀 INICIANDO CLUSTER DE AGENTES DE ARBITRAJE FLASH LOAN V2 (OPTIMIZADO)');
  logger.info(`   Agentes Concurrentes: ${SWARM_AGENTS.length} Agentes Especializados`);
  logger.info(`   Pares de Liquidez Totales: ${MULTI_CHAIN_ARBITRAGE_PAIRS.length} Pares Multi-Cadena`);
  logger.info(`   Modo DRY_RUN: ${ENV.dryRun ? 'ACTIVADO (Simulación sin riesgo)' : 'DESACTIVADO (MODO LIVE MAINNET REAL)'}`);
  logger.info(`   Ganancia Mínima Objetivo: $${ENV.minProfitUSD.toFixed(2)} USD`);
  logger.info(`   Optimizaciones MEV: Sweet Spot Loan Search | Balancer 0% Fee | Arbitrum Sequencer Feed | Base Flashblocks`);
  logger.divider();

  // 1. Inicializar Gestor de RPCs y cargar Chainlist.org
  const rpcManager = new RPCManager();
  await rpcManager.loadChainlistRPCs();

  // 2. Monitoreo en Vivo de L1 Data Fee (EIP-4844 Blobs)
  const baseProvider = rpcManager.getProvider(8453);
  const arbProvider = rpcManager.getProvider(42161);
  await SweetSpotOptimizer.updateL1GasFees(baseProvider, arbProvider);
  logger.info(`⛽ [L1 DATA FEE MONITOR] Parámetros EIP-4844 actualizados (Base L1: ${SweetSpotOptimizer.liveL1BaseFeeBase} wei, Arb L1: ${SweetSpotOptimizer.liveL1BaseFeeArb} wei)`);

  setInterval(() => {
    SweetSpotOptimizer.updateL1GasFees(baseProvider, arbProvider).catch(() => {});
  }, 30000);

  // 3. Garantizar autorizaciones de Uniswap Permit2 y preparar Ejecutores
  const permit2Manager = new Permit2ApprovalManager(rpcManager);
  await permit2Manager.ensureAllPermit2Approvals().catch((e) => {
    logger.warn(`Fallo no-bloqueante en Permit2 approvals: ${e.message}`);
  });

  const { spotExecutor } = require('./executor/spotExecutor');
  await spotExecutor.initialize().catch((e: any) => {
    logger.warn(`Fallo no-bloqueante en Spot Executor: ${e.message}`);
  });

  const { arbitrumExecutor } = require('./executor/arbitrumExecutor');
  await arbitrumExecutor.initialize().catch((e: any) => {
    logger.warn(`Fallo no-bloqueante en Arbitrum Executor: ${e.message}`);
  });

  const { lowFeeExecutor } = require('./executor/lowFeeExecutor');
  await lowFeeExecutor.initialize().catch((e: any) => {
    logger.warn(`Fallo no-bloqueante en Low-Fee Executor: ${e.message}`);
  });

  // Benchmark preliminar para TODAS las cadenas
  logger.info('Iniciando benchmark de latencia RPC para todas las cadenas soportadas...');
  for (const chainIdStr of Object.keys(SUPPORTED_CHAINS)) {
    const chainId = parseInt(chainIdStr, 10);
    await rpcManager.benchmarkAndSelectBestRPC(chainId).catch(() => {});
    rpcManager.subscribeNewHeads(chainId, (blockNumber) => {
      // Disparo inmediato por WebSocket en milisegundo 0
    });
  }

  // 4. Inicializar Swarm Cluster de Agentes
  const swarmManager = new SwarmManager(rpcManager);
  setSwarmManager(swarmManager);

  // 5. Conexión de Infraestructura MEV de Alta Velocidad
  // Arbitrum Nitro Sequencer Feed (sub-10ms)
  const arbSequencerFeed = new ArbitrumSequencerFeedListener(
    ENV.arbitrumWssUrl || 'wss://arb1.arbitrum.io/feed',
    (batchCount) => {
      swarmManager.triggerChainScan(42161).catch(() => {});
    }
  );
  arbSequencerFeed.start();

  // Base Flashblocks / Bloques Rápidos
  const baseFlashblockFeed = new BaseFlashblockFeedListener(
    ENV.baseWssUrl || 'wss://base-rpc.publicnode.com',
    (blockNum) => {
      swarmManager.triggerChainScan(8453).catch(() => {});
    }
  );
  baseFlashblockFeed.start();

  // 6. Iniciar Servidor de API HTTP
  startServer();

  // 7. Notificar a Telegram
  await telegramNotifier.notifyStartup(MULTI_CHAIN_ARBITRAGE_PAIRS.length, SWARM_AGENTS.length).catch(() => {});

  // 8. Arquitectura MEV Reactiva por Eventos
  // Los escaneos son disparados en tiempo real por el Sequencer Feed de Arbitrum (<10ms)
  // y los Flashblocks de Base (200ms) sin sobrecargar RPCs ni generar telemetría falsa.
  // Para activar el bucle continuo tradicional del Swarm:
  // await swarmManager.startSwarmLoop();
  logger.success(`🔥 Motores de Arbitraje Activos: Escuchando eventos de Sequencer Feed y Flashblocks.`);
}

main().catch((err) => {
  logger.error(`Fallo crítico al iniciar el cluster de agentes: ${err.message}`);
  process.exit(1);
});

export let globalSwarmManager: SwarmManager | null = null;
export const LIVE_PAIR_RESULTS: Record<string, any> = {};

export const RECENT_SCAN_LOGS: string[] = [];
export const SPOT_BASE_LOGS: string[] = [];
export const FLASH_LOAN_LOGS: string[] = [];
export const LOW_FEE_LOGS: string[] = [];

export function addScanLog(msg: string, category?: string) {
  const timestamp = new Date().toLocaleTimeString();
  const formatted = `[${timestamp}] ${msg}`;
  
  RECENT_SCAN_LOGS.unshift(formatted);
  if (RECENT_SCAN_LOGS.length > 50) RECENT_SCAN_LOGS.pop();

  if (category === 'LOW_FEE' || msg.includes('LOW-FEE') || msg.includes('0.05%') || msg.includes('0.01%')) {
    LOW_FEE_LOGS.unshift(formatted);
    if (LOW_FEE_LOGS.length > 50) LOW_FEE_LOGS.pop();
  } else if (category === 'SPOT_BASE' || msg.includes('SPOT') || msg.includes('TRIANGULAR') || msg.includes('Base') || msg.includes('8453')) {
    SPOT_BASE_LOGS.unshift(formatted);
    if (SPOT_BASE_LOGS.length > 50) SPOT_BASE_LOGS.pop();
  } else {
    FLASH_LOAN_LOGS.unshift(formatted);
    if (FLASH_LOAN_LOGS.length > 50) FLASH_LOAN_LOGS.pop();
  }
}

export function setSwarmManager(manager: SwarmManager) {
  globalSwarmManager = manager;
  streamProcessor.setSwarmManager(manager);
}

export function addSpotOpportunity(opp: any) {
  opp.type = 'SPOT_BASE';
  opp.chainId = 8453;
  opp.timestamp = Date.now();

  const idx = SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities.findIndex(o => o.pairName === opp.pairName);
  if (idx >= 0) {
    SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities[idx] = opp;
  } else {
    SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities.unshift(opp);
    if (SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities.length > 25) SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities.pop();
  }
  updateMergedOpportunities();
}

export function addLowFeeOpportunity(opp: any) {
  opp.type = 'LOW_FEE';
  opp.timestamp = Date.now();

  const idx = SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities.findIndex(o => o.pairName === opp.pairName && o.chainId === opp.chainId);
  if (idx >= 0) {
    SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities[idx] = opp;
  } else {
    SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities.unshift(opp);
    if (SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities.length > 25) SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities.pop();
  }
  updateMergedOpportunities();
}

export function addFlashOpportunity(opp: any) {
  opp.type = 'FLASH_LOAN';
  opp.timestamp = Date.now();

  const idx = SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities.findIndex(o => o.pairName === opp.pairName && o.chainId === opp.chainId);
  if (idx >= 0) {
    SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities[idx] = opp;
  } else {
    SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities.unshift(opp);
    if (SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities.length > 25) SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities.pop();
  }
  updateMergedOpportunities();
}

function updateMergedOpportunities() {
  SERVER_STATS.lastOpportunities = [
    ...SERVER_STATS.lowFeeOpportunities,
    ...SERVER_STATS.spotOpportunities,
    ...SERVER_STATS.flashOpportunities
  ];
}

app.post('/api/mempool-stream', (req, res) => {
  try {
    const payload: StreamPayload = req.body;
    if (payload && payload.eventType) {
      streamProcessor.processStreamEvent(payload);
      addScanLog(`⚡ [QUICKNODE STREAM] Evento ${payload.eventType} recibido para ${payload.pairName || 'Pool'}`, 'FLASH_LOAN');
      return res.status(200).json({ success: true, processed: true });
    }
    return res.status(200).json({ success: true, status: 'CONNECTED' });
  } catch (err: any) {
    logger.warn(`Error al procesar payload de QuickNode Stream: ${err.message}`);
    return res.status(200).json({ success: true, status: 'CONNECTED' });
  }
});

app.get('/api/agents', (req, res) => {
  const regiment = req.query.regiment ? parseInt(req.query.regiment as string, 10) : undefined;
  const limit = req.query.limit ? parseInt(req.query.limit as string, 10) : 100;
  if (globalSwarmManager) {
    res.json(globalSwarmManager.getClusterStatus(regiment, limit));
  } else {
    res.json([]);
  }
});

app.get('/api/pairs/live', (req, res) => {
  res.json(LIVE_PAIR_RESULTS);
});

app.get('/api/logs', (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    spotLogs: SPOT_BASE_LOGS,
    flashLogs: FLASH_LOAN_LOGS,
    recentLogs: RECENT_SCAN_LOGS
  });
});

app.get('/api/pairs', (req, res) => {
  res.json(MULTI_CHAIN_ARBITRAGE_PAIRS);
});

app.get('/api/dexs', (req, res) => {
  res.json(DEX_PROTOCOLS);
});

app.get('/api/cex-lag', (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    eth: cexLagPredictor.getCEXPrice('ETH'),
    btc: cexLagPredictor.getCEXPrice('BTC'),
    sol: cexLagPredictor.getCEXPrice('SOL'),
    arb: cexLagPredictor.getCEXPrice('ARB'),
    op: cexLagPredictor.getCEXPrice('OP')
  });
});

export function startServer(initialPort: number = ENV.port) {
  loadPersistedStats();
  cexLagPredictor.startMonitoring(2000).catch(() => {});
  binanceWebSocketFeed.start();
  const server = app.listen(initialPort, () => {
    logger.info(`🌐 Servidor de monitoreo V2 ejecutándose en http://localhost:${initialPort}`);
    logger.info(`   -> Dashboard Web: http://localhost:${initialPort}`);
    logger.info(`   -> Endpoint Webhook QuickNode Streams: http://localhost:${initialPort}/api/mempool-stream`);
  });

  server.on('error', (err: any) => {
    if (err.code === 'EADDRINUSE') {
      const nextPort = initialPort + 1;
      logger.warn(`Puerto ${initialPort} ocupado. Intentando en http://localhost:${nextPort}...`);
      startServer(nextPort);
    } else {
      logger.error(`Error en servidor API: ${err.message}`);
    }
  });
}

if (require.main === module) {
  startServer();
}

import axios from 'axios';
import { ENV } from '../config/env';
import { ArbitrageOpportunity } from '../protocolink/protocolinkClient';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';

export class TelegramNotifier {
  private botToken: string;
  private chatId: string;
  private isConfigured: boolean;

  constructor() {
    this.botToken = process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN || '';
    this.chatId = process.env.TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID || '';
    this.isConfigured = Boolean(this.botToken && this.chatId);
  }

  /**
   * Enviar mensaje simple o formateado a Telegram
   */
  async sendMessage(text: string): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!this.isConfigured) return false;

    try {
      const url = `https://api.telegram.org/bot${this.botToken}/sendMessage`;
      await axios.post(url, {
        chat_id: this.chatId,
        text,
        parse_mode: 'HTML',
        disable_web_page_preview: true,
      }, { timeout: 5000 });
      return true;
    } catch (err: any) {
      logger.warn(`[TELEGRAM ALERT] Error al enviar mensaje: ${err.message}`);
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Notificación cuando se ejecuta con éxito un arbitraje on-chain
   */
  async notifyExecution(opp: ArbitrageOpportunity, agentName: string, txHash?: string): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.isConfigured) return;

    const chainMap: Record<number, { name: string; explorer: string }> = {
      1: { name: 'Ethereum Mainnet', explorer: 'https://etherscan.io/tx/' },
      42161: { name: 'Arbitrum One', explorer: 'https://arbiscan.io/tx/' },
      8453: { name: 'Base Mainnet', explorer: 'https://basescan.org/tx/' },
      10: { name: 'Optimism Mainnet', explorer: 'https://optimistic.etherscan.io/tx/' },
    };

    const chainInfo = chainMap[opp.chainId] || { name: `Chain ${opp.chainId}`, explorer: '' };
    const txLink = txHash && chainInfo.explorer ? `<a href="${chainInfo.explorer}${txHash}">Ver en Explorador</a>` : 'Confirmada On-Chain';

    const message = `
🚀 <b>¡ARBITRAJE FLASH LOAN EJECUTADO CON ÉXITO!</b> 🚀

🤖 <b>Agente:</b> ${agentName}
🌐 <b>Red:</b> ${chainInfo.name}
💎 <b>Par:</b> ${opp.pairName}
💰 <b>Ganancia Neta:</b> <code>+$${opp.netProfitUSD.toFixed(2)} USD</code>

📦 <b>Ruta de Arbitraje:</b>
 • <b>Paso 1:</b> Comprar en <b>${opp.leg1.protocolId.toUpperCase()}</b>
 • <b>Paso 2:</b> Vender en <b>${opp.leg2.protocolId.toUpperCase()}</b>
 • <b>Préstamo Flash:</b> ${opp.flashLoanAmount} ${opp.tokenA.symbol}

⛽ <b>Costo Gas:</b> $${opp.estimatedGasUSD.toFixed(2)} USD
🔗 <b>Transacción:</b> ${txLink}
⏰ <b>Hora:</b> ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}
`;

    await this.sendMessage(message);
  }

  /**
   * Notificación cuando se inicia o reinicia el cluster
   */
  async notifyStartup(pairsCount: number, agentsCount: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.isConfigured) return;

    const msg = `
🟢 <b>CLUSTER DE ARBITRAJE INICIADO 24/7</b>

🖥️ <b>Servidor VPS:</b> Contabo Cloud (8 vCPUs)
⚡ <b>Nodos:</b> QuickNode Dedicated WSS Streams (< 2ms)
📊 <b>Pares Monitoreados:</b> ${pairsCount} pares activos
🤖 <b>Agentes Swarm:</b> ${agentsCount} agentes concurrentes
🎯 <b>Optimización:</b> Sweet Spot (x*y=k) Activado
🌐 <b>Dashboard:</b> http://217.77.14.31:3000
`;
    await this.sendMessage(msg);
  }
}

export const telegramNotifier = new TelegramNotifier();

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { SERVER_STATS } from '../server';
import { logger } from './logger';

const STATS_FILE = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data', 'stats.json');

export function loadPersistedStats(): void {
  try {
    const dataDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data');
    if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, { recursive: true });
    }

    if (fs.existsSync(STATS_FILE)) {
      const content = fs.readFileSync(STATS_FILE, 'utf-8');
      const saved = JSON.parse(content);
      if (typeof saved.detectedOpportunitiesCount === 'number') {
        SERVER_STATS.detectedOpportunitiesCount = saved.detectedOpportunitiesCount;
      }
      if (typeof saved.detectedPnLUSD === 'number') {
        SERVER_STATS.detectedPnLUSD = saved.detectedPnLUSD;
      }
      if (typeof saved.onChainExecutedCount === 'number') {
        SERVER_STATS.onChainExecutedCount = saved.onChainExecutedCount;
      }
      if (typeof saved.onChainRealizedPnLUSD === 'number') {
        SERVER_STATS.onChainRealizedPnLUSD = saved.onChainRealizedPnLUSD;
      }
      if (typeof saved.opportunitiesFound === 'number') {
        SERVER_STATS.opportunitiesFound = saved.opportunitiesFound;
      }
      if (typeof saved.scansCompleted === 'number') {
        SERVER_STATS.scansCompleted = saved.scansCompleted;
      }
      logger.info(`💾 Estadísticas cargadas desde disco: ${SERVER_STATS.detectedOpportunitiesCount} Oportunidades Detectadas ($${SERVER_STATS.detectedPnLUSD.toFixed(2)}) | On-Chain: ${SERVER_STATS.onChainExecutedCount} Transacciones ($${SERVER_STATS.onChainRealizedPnLUSD.toFixed(2)})`);
    }
  } catch (err: any) {
    logger.warn(`No se pudieron cargar las estadísticas guardadas: ${err.message}`);
  }
}

export function savePersistedStats(): void {
  try {
    const dataDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data');
    if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, { recursive: true });
    }

    const payload = {
      detectedOpportunitiesCount: SERVER_STATS.detectedOpportunitiesCount,
      detectedPnLUSD: SERVER_STATS.detectedPnLUSD,
      onChainExecutedCount: SERVER_STATS.onChainExecutedCount,
      onChainRealizedPnLUSD: SERVER_STATS.onChainRealizedPnLUSD,
      opportunitiesFound: SERVER_STATS.opportunitiesFound,
      scansCompleted: SERVER_STATS.scansCompleted,
      lastUpdated: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    fs.writeFileSync(STATS_FILE, JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), 'utf-8');
  } catch (err: any) {
    logger.warn(`No se pudieron guardar las estadísticas en disco: ${err.message}`);
  }
}



███████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████
██ ARCHIVO: src/utils/historyStore.ts
██ RUTA ABSOLUTA: C:\Users\Jose\Desktop\PELI_TRIAUDIO\flashloan-arbitrage-agent-v2\src\utils\historyStore.ts
███████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { logger } from './logger';

export interface OpportunityHistoryRecord {
  id: string;
  timestamp: string;
  chainId: number;
  pairName: string;
  leg1Protocol: string;
  leg2Protocol: string;
  inputAmount: string;
  outputAmount: string;
  grossProfitUSD: number;
  netProfitUSD: number;
  isExecuted: boolean;
  txHash?: string;
}

export interface WeeklySummaryStats {
  weekPeriod: string;
  totalScans: number;
  totalOpportunitiesDetected: number;
  totalDetectedPnLUSD: number;
  totalOnChainExecuted: number;
  totalRealizedPnLUSD: number;
  bestOpportunityUSD: number;
  mostVolatilePair: string;
  chainBreakdown: {
    arbitrum: number;
    base: number;
    ethereum: number;
    optimism: number;
  };
  dailyPnLHistory: { date: string; pnlUSD: number; count: number }[];
  top10Pools: { name: string; chainId: number; count: number; avgSpreadUSD: number }[];
}

export class HistoryStore {
  private filePath: string;
  private records: OpportunityHistoryRecord[] = [];

  constructor() {
    const dataDir = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data');
    if (!fs.existsSync(dataDir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(dataDir, { recursive: true });
    }
    this.filePath = path.join(dataDir, 'arbitrage_history.json');
    this.loadFromDisk();
  }

  private loadFromDisk() {
    try {
      if (fs.existsSync(this.filePath)) {
        const raw = fs.readFileSync(this.filePath, 'utf-8');
        this.records = JSON.parse(raw);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      logger.warn(`Error al cargar historial desde disco: ${err.message}`);
      this.records = [];
    }
  }

  saveToDisk() {
    try {
      fs.writeFileSync(this.filePath, JSON.stringify(this.records, null, 2), 'utf-8');
    } catch (err: any) {
      logger.warn(`Error al guardar historial en disco: ${err.message}`);
    }
  }

  addRecord(opp: any, isExecuted: boolean = false, txHash?: string) {
    const record: OpportunityHistoryRecord = {
      id: opp.id || `OPP-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 5)}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      chainId: opp.chainId || 42161,
      pairName: opp.pairName || opp.cycleName || 'Unknown Pair',
      leg1Protocol: opp.leg1?.protocolId || 'UNISWAP-V3',
      leg2Protocol: opp.leg2?.protocolId || 'CAMELOT-V3',
      inputAmount: opp.leg1?.inputAmount || '0',
      outputAmount: opp.leg2?.outputAmount || '0',
      grossProfitUSD: opp.grossProfitUSD || 0,
      netProfitUSD: opp.netProfitUSD || 0,
      isExecuted,
      txHash
    };

    this.records.unshift(record);
    // Mantener hasta 5,000 registros históricos en memoria
    if (this.records.length > 5000) {
      this.records.pop();
    }
    this.saveToDisk();
  }

  clearHistory() {
    this.records = [];
    this.saveToDisk();
  }

  getWeeklySummary(totalScansCompleted: number = 0): WeeklySummaryStats {
    const now = new Date();
    const sevenDaysAgo = new Date(now.getTime() - 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);

    const weekRecords = this.records.filter(r => new Date(r.timestamp) >= sevenDaysAgo);

    let totalDetectedPnLUSD = 0;
    let totalOnChainExecuted = 0;
    let totalRealizedPnLUSD = 0;
    let bestOpportunityUSD = 0;

    const chainCounts = { arbitrum: 0, base: 0, ethereum: 0, optimism: 0 };
    const pairCounts: Record<string, { chainId: number; count: number; totalProfit: number }> = {};
    const dailyMap: Record<string, { pnlUSD: number; count: number }> = {};

    // Inicializar los últimos 7 días
    for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
      const d = new Date(now.getTime() - i * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      const dateStr = d.toISOString().split('T')[0];
      dailyMap[dateStr] = { pnlUSD: 0, count: 0 };
    }

    for (const r of weekRecords) {
      totalDetectedPnLUSD += r.netProfitUSD;
      if (r.netProfitUSD > bestOpportunityUSD) {
        bestOpportunityUSD = r.netProfitUSD;
      }
      if (r.isExecuted) {
        totalOnChainExecuted++;
        totalRealizedPnLUSD += r.netProfitUSD;
      }

      // Conteo por cadena
      if (r.chainId === 42161) chainCounts.arbitrum++;
      else if (r.chainId === 8453) chainCounts.base++;
      else if (r.chainId === 1) chainCounts.ethereum++;
      else if (r.chainId === 10) chainCounts.optimism++;

      // Conteo por par
      if (!pairCounts[r.pairName]) {
        pairCounts[r.pairName] = { chainId: r.chainId, count: 0, totalProfit: 0 };
      }
      pairCounts[r.pairName].count++;
      pairCounts[r.pairName].totalProfit += r.netProfitUSD;

      // Agrupación diaria
      const dateKey = r.timestamp.split('T')[0];
      if (dailyMap[dateKey]) {
        dailyMap[dateKey].pnlUSD += r.netProfitUSD;
        dailyMap[dateKey].count++;
      }
    }

    // Calcular par más volátil
    let mostVolatilePair = 'N/A';
    let maxPairCount = 0;
    for (const [name, data] of Object.entries(pairCounts)) {
      if (data.count > maxPairCount) {
        maxPairCount = data.count;
        mostVolatilePair = name;
      }
    }

    // Top 10 pools
    const top10Pools = Object.entries(pairCounts)
      .map(([name, data]) => ({
        name,
        chainId: data.chainId,
        count: data.count,
        avgSpreadUSD: data.count > 0 ? data.totalProfit / data.count : 0
      }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count)
      .slice(0, 10);

    const dailyPnLHistory = Object.entries(dailyMap).map(([date, data]) => ({
      date,
      pnlUSD: Number(data.pnlUSD.toFixed(2)),
      count: data.count
    }));

    return {
      weekPeriod: `${sevenDaysAgo.toLocaleDateString()} - ${now.toLocaleDateString()}`,
      totalScans: totalScansCompleted,
      totalOpportunitiesDetected: weekRecords.length,
      totalDetectedPnLUSD: Number(totalDetectedPnLUSD.toFixed(2)),
      totalOnChainExecuted,
      totalRealizedPnLUSD: Number(totalRealizedPnLUSD.toFixed(2)),
      bestOpportunityUSD: Number(bestOpportunityUSD.toFixed(2)),
      mostVolatilePair,
      chainBreakdown: chainCounts,
      dailyPnLHistory,
      top10Pools
    };
  }
}

export const historyStore = new HistoryStore();

import { ethers } from 'ethers';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { SweetSpotOptimizer } from '../src/math/sweetSpotOptimizer';
import { LENDING_POOLS } from '../src/liquidations/lendingRegistry';
import { BASE_100_TOKENS } from '../src/config/baseTokens100';
import { spotExecutor } from '../src/executor/spotExecutor';

async function runPhasesVerification() {
  console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════');
  console.log('🧪 SUITE DE PRUEBAS DE VERIFICACIÓN COMPLETA (FASES 1 A 5 AUDITADAS)');
  console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n');

  let passedTests = 0;
  let totalTests = 0;

  function assert(condition: boolean, testName: string, detail: string = '') {
    totalTests++;
    if (condition) {
      console.log(`  ✅ [PASS] ${testName}`);
      if (detail) console.log(`     └─ ${detail}`);
      passedTests++;
    } else {
      console.error(`  ❌ [FAIL] ${testName}`);
      if (detail) console.error(`     └─ ERROR: ${detail}`);
    }
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // FASE 1: Smart Contracts Blindados con Retornos de Ganancia
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  console.log('🛡️ FASE 1: Verificación de Smart Contracts Blindados');

  const baseArtifactPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'artifacts', 'contracts', 'BaseUniversalArbitrage.sol', 'BaseUniversalArbitrage.json');
  const arbArtifactPath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'artifacts', 'contracts', 'ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage.sol', 'ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage.json');

  assert(fs.existsSync(baseArtifactPath), 'Artifact de BaseUniversalArbitrage existe');
  assert(fs.existsSync(arbArtifactPath), 'Artifact de ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage existe');

  if (fs.existsSync(baseArtifactPath)) {
    const baseArtifact = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(baseArtifactPath, 'utf8'));
    const abi = baseArtifact.abi;
    const executeBalancerSpatial = abi.find((i: any) => i.name === 'executeBalancerFlashLoanSpatial');
    const hasOutputs = executeBalancerSpatial && executeBalancerSpatial.outputs && executeBalancerSpatial.outputs.length > 0;
    assert(hasOutputs, 'BaseUniversalArbitrage: executeBalancerFlashLoanSpatial tiene returns (uint256 profit)');

    const executeAaveSpatial = abi.find((i: any) => i.name === 'executeFlashLoanSpatial');
    const aaveHasOutputs = executeAaveSpatial && executeAaveSpatial.outputs && executeAaveSpatial.outputs.length > 0;
    assert(aaveHasOutputs, 'BaseUniversalArbitrage: executeFlashLoanSpatial tiene returns (uint256 profit)');
  }

  if (fs.existsSync(arbArtifactPath)) {
    const arbArtifact = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(arbArtifactPath, 'utf8'));
    const abi = arbArtifact.abi;
    const executeBalancerSpatialArb = abi.find((i: any) => i.name === 'executeBalancerFlashLoanSpatial');
    const hasOutputsArb = executeBalancerSpatialArb && executeBalancerSpatialArb.outputs && executeBalancerSpatialArb.outputs.length > 0;
    assert(hasOutputsArb, 'ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage: executeBalancerFlashLoanSpatial tiene returns (uint256 profit)');
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // FASE 2: Purga Absoluta de Fórmulas Ficticias en Ejecutores
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  console.log('\n🧹 FASE 2: Verificación de Eliminación de Humo en Ejecutores');

  const executorFiles = ['spotExecutor.ts', 'arbitrumExecutor.ts', 'lowFeeExecutor.ts'];
  for (const file of executorFiles) {
    const filePath = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'executor', file);
    if (fs.existsSync(filePath)) {
      const content = fs.readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
      const hasMathSin = /Math\.(sin|cos)/.test(content);
      assert(!hasMathSin, `src/executor/${file} no contiene fórmulas sinusoidales (Math.sin/cos)`);
      const hasEstimateGas = content.includes('estimateGas');
      assert(hasEstimateGas, `src/executor/${file} implementa pre-flight check dinámico con estimateGas`);
    }
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // FASE 3: Saneamiento de Datos, Tokens y Métodos de Agente
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  console.log('\n💎 FASE 3: Verificación de Tokens Verificados y Puentes');

  let invalidAddressesCount = 0;
  for (const token of BASE_100_TOKENS) {
    if (!token.address.startsWith('0x') || token.address.length !== 42 || !/^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$/.test(token.address)) {
      invalidAddressesCount++;
    }
  }
  assert(invalidAddressesCount === 0, `Todos los tokens en baseTokens100.ts son direcciones hex válidas (${BASE_100_TOKENS.length} tokens verificados)`);

  const hasExecuteSpotMethod = typeof (spotExecutor as any).executeSpot === 'function';
  assert(hasExecuteSpotMethod, 'SpotExecutor expone el método puente executeSpot(opp) para compatibilidad con agentWorker');

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // FASE 4: Unificación Matemática y L1 Data Fee Real
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  console.log('\n⛽ FASE 4: Verificación de Gas Real (L1 Data Fee + L2 Execution)');

  const duplicatedFile = path.join(__dirname, '..', 'src', 'agent', 'sweetSpotOptimizer.ts');
  assert(!fs.existsSync(duplicatedFile), 'Archivo duplicado src/agent/sweetSpotOptimizer.ts eliminado');

  const baseGasCalc = SweetSpotOptimizer.calculateTotalGasCostUSD(8453, 200_000n, 0.05, 2500, 384);
  assert(baseGasCalc.l1DataFeeUSD > 0, 'Base L1 Calldata Fee correctamente calculado (> $0.001 USD)');

  const microArbResult = SweetSpotOptimizer.calculateOptimalLoan(1000, 0.0001, 1.0, baseGasCalc.totalGasUSD, 2500, 0.0, 0.01);
  assert(!microArbResult.isViable, 'Filtro anti-sub-centavo: spread menor al costo de L1 Data Fee es rechazado');

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // FASE 5: Rendimiento Flash Loan 0% Balancer vs 5 bps Aave
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  console.log('\n📊 FASE 5: Comparativa de Rendimiento Balancer (0% Fee) vs Aave (0.05%)');

  const balancerResult = SweetSpotOptimizer.calculateOptimalLoan(5000, 0.0035, 2000, baseGasCalc.totalGasUSD, 2500, 0.0, 0.01);
  const aaveResult = SweetSpotOptimizer.calculateOptimalLoan(5000, 0.0035, 2000, baseGasCalc.totalGasUSD, 2500, 0.0005, 0.01);

  assert(
    balancerResult.expectedNetProfitUSD > aaveResult.expectedNetProfitUSD,
    'Balancer V2 retiene mayor ganancia neta al tener 0% fee frente a Aave (0.05%)',
    `Balancer: $${balancerResult.expectedNetProfitUSD} | Aave: $${aaveResult.expectedNetProfitUSD}`
  );

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // RESUMEN FINAL
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  console.log('\n═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════');
  console.log(`🏁 RESULTADO: ${passedTests} / ${totalTests} Pruebas Exitosas (${((passedTests / totalTests) * 100).toFixed(1)}%)`);
  console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n');

  if (passedTests !== totalTests) {
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

runPhasesVerification().catch((e) => {
  console.error('Fallo en la suite de pruebas:', e);
  process.exit(1);
});

import { ethers, network } from 'hardhat';

async function main() {
  console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════');
  console.log('🔬 SUITE DE SIMULACIÓN EN FORK REAL: BASE & ARBITRUM ONE');
  console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n');

  let passed = 0;
  let total = 0;

  function assert(condition: boolean, title: string, detail: string = '') {
    total++;
    if (condition) {
      console.log(`  ✅ [PASS] ${title}`);
      if (detail) console.log(`     └─ ${detail}`);
      passed++;
    } else {
      console.error(`  ❌ [FAIL] ${title}`);
      if (detail) console.error(`     └─ ERROR: ${detail}`);
    }
  }

  const [owner, attacker] = await ethers.getSigners();

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // 1. FORK TEST EN BASE MAINNET (ChainID 8453)
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  console.log('🔵 INICIANDO FORK EN BASE MAINNET (https://mainnet.base.org)...');
  try {
    await network.provider.request({
      method: 'hardhat_reset',
      params: [
        {
          forking: {
            jsonRpcUrl: process.env.BASE_RPC_URL || 'https://mainnet.base.org',
          },
        },
      ],
    });
    console.log('   └─ Fork de Base conectado correctamente.\n');

    console.log('📦 Desplegando BaseUniversalArbitrage en el Fork...');
    const BaseFactory = await ethers.getContractFactory('BaseUniversalArbitrage', owner);
    const baseContract = await BaseFactory.deploy();
    await baseContract.waitForDeployment();
    const baseAddress = await baseContract.getAddress();
    assert(!!baseAddress && baseAddress.startsWith('0x'), 'BaseUniversalArbitrage desplegado en Fork de Base', `Dirección: ${baseAddress}`);

    // Pre-aprobar WETH y USDC
    const WETH_BASE = '0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006';
    const USDC_BASE = '0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913';
    await baseContract.approveRouters([WETH_BASE, USDC_BASE]);
    assert(true, 'approveRouters() ejecutado con éxito en Base');

    // PRUEBA DE SEGURIDAD 1: Intento de Secuestro de Callback en Balancer V2
    console.log('\n🛡️ Probando Seguridad de Callback Balancer V2 en Base...');
    const BALANCER_VAULT_BASE = '0xBA12222222228d8Ba445958a75a0704d566BF2C8';
    let hijackedBalancer = false;
    try {
      await network.provider.request({
        method: 'hardhat_impersonateAccount',
        params: [BALANCER_VAULT_BASE],
      });
      await owner.sendTransaction({ to: BALANCER_VAULT_BASE, value: ethers.parseEther('1.0') });
      const vaultSigner = await ethers.getSigner(BALANCER_VAULT_BASE);

      const dummyRoute = {
        tokenIn: WETH_BASE,
        tokenIntermediate: USDC_BASE,
        dexBuy: 0,
        dexSell: 1,
        feeOrSpacingBuy: 500,
        feeOrSpacingSell: 100,
        minProfit: 1000n,
      };
      const dummyUserData = ethers.AbiCoder.defaultAbiCoder().encode(['uint8', 'bytes'], [2, ethers.AbiCoder.defaultAbiCoder().encode([
        'tuple(address,address,uint8,uint8,uint24,uint24,uint256)'
      ], [[dummyRoute.tokenIn, dummyRoute.tokenIntermediate, dummyRoute.dexBuy, dummyRoute.dexSell, dummyRoute.feeOrSpacingBuy, dummyRoute.feeOrSpacingSell, dummyRoute.minProfit]])]);

      await (baseContract.connect(vaultSigner) as any).receiveFlashLoan(
        [WETH_BASE],
        [ethers.parseEther('1.0')],
        [0n],
        dummyUserData
      );
      hijackedBalancer = true;
    } catch (err: any) {
      hijackedBalancer = false;
      assert(
        err.message.toLowerCase().includes('revert'),
        'Guardia _inFlashLoan bloqueó exitosamente callback no autorizado de Balancer',
        `Reversión confirmada: ${err.message.slice(0, 80)}...`
      );
    }
    assert(!hijackedBalancer, 'Atacante NO puede explotar receiveFlashLoan');

    // PRUEBA DE SEGURIDAD 2: Intento de Secuestro de Callback en Aave V3
    console.log('\n🛡️ Probando Seguridad de Callback Aave V3 en Base...');
    const AAVE_POOL_BASE = '0xA238Dd80C259a72e81d7e4664a9801593F98d1c5';
    let hijackedAave = false;
    try {
      await network.provider.request({
        method: 'hardhat_impersonateAccount',
        params: [AAVE_POOL_BASE],
      });
      await owner.sendTransaction({ to: AAVE_POOL_BASE, value: ethers.parseEther('1.0') });
      const aaveSigner = await ethers.getSigner(AAVE_POOL_BASE);

      await (baseContract.connect(aaveSigner) as any).executeOperation(
        WETH_BASE,
        ethers.parseEther('1.0'),
        ethers.parseEther('0.0005'),
        attacker.address, // Initiator malicioso
        '0x'
      );
      hijackedAave = true;
    } catch (err: any) {
      hijackedAave = false;
      assert(
        err.message.toLowerCase().includes('revert'),
        'Guardia de Initiator bloqueó exitosamente callback manipulado de Aave',
        `Reversión confirmada: ${err.message.slice(0, 80)}...`
      );
    }
    assert(!hijackedAave, 'Atacante NO puede forzar executeOperation');

  } catch (baseErr: any) {
    console.error('⚠️ Error en Fork de Base:', baseErr.message);
  }

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // 2. FORK TEST EN ARBITRUM ONE (ChainID 42161)
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  console.log('\n🔴 INICIANDO FORK EN ARBITRUM ONE (https://arb1.arbitrum.io/rpc)...');
  try {
    await network.provider.request({
      method: 'hardhat_reset',
      params: [
        {
          forking: {
            jsonRpcUrl: process.env.ARBITRUM_RPC_URL || 'https://arb1.arbitrum.io/rpc',
          },
        },
      ],
    });
    console.log('   └─ Fork de Arbitrum One conectado correctamente.\n');

    console.log('📦 Desplegando ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage en el Fork...');
    const ArbFactory = await ethers.getContractFactory('ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage', owner);
    const arbContract = await ArbFactory.deploy();
    await arbContract.waitForDeployment();
    const arbAddress = await arbContract.getAddress();
    assert(!!arbAddress && arbAddress.startsWith('0x'), 'ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage desplegado en Fork de Arbitrum', `Dirección: ${arbAddress}`);

    const WETH_ARB = '0x82aF49447D8a07e3bd95BD0d56f35241523fBab1';
    const USDC_ARB = '0xaf88d065e77c8cC2239327C5EDb3A432268e5831';
    await arbContract.approveRouters([WETH_ARB, USDC_ARB]);
    assert(true, 'approveRouters() ejecutado con éxito en Arbitrum');

    // PRUEBA DE SEGURIDAD 3: Intento de Secuestro de Callback en Arbitrum Balancer V2
    console.log('\n🛡️ Probando Seguridad de Callback Balancer V2 en Arbitrum...');
    const BALANCER_VAULT_ARB = '0xBA12222222228d8Ba445958a75a0704d566BF2C8';
    let hijackedArbBalancer = false;
    try {
      await network.provider.request({
        method: 'hardhat_impersonateAccount',
        params: [BALANCER_VAULT_ARB],
      });
      await owner.sendTransaction({ to: BALANCER_VAULT_ARB, value: ethers.parseEther('1.0') });
      const vaultArbSigner = await ethers.getSigner(BALANCER_VAULT_ARB);

      const dummyUserDataArb = ethers.AbiCoder.defaultAbiCoder().encode(['uint8', 'bytes'], [0, '0x']);
      await (arbContract.connect(vaultArbSigner) as any).receiveFlashLoan(
        [WETH_ARB],
        [ethers.parseEther('1.0')],
        [0n],
        dummyUserDataArb
      );
      hijackedArbBalancer = true;
    } catch (err: any) {
      hijackedArbBalancer = false;
      assert(
        err.message.toLowerCase().includes('revert'),
        'Guardia _inFlashLoan bloqueó exitosamente callback de Balancer en Arbitrum',
        `Reversión confirmada: ${err.message.slice(0, 80)}...`
      );
    }
    assert(!hijackedArbBalancer, 'Atacante NO puede explotar receiveFlashLoan en Arbitrum');

    // PRUEBA DE SEGURIDAD 4: Intento de Secuestro de Callback en Arbitrum Aave V3
    console.log('\n🛡️ Probando Seguridad de Callback Aave V3 en Arbitrum...');
    const AAVE_POOL_ARB = '0x794a61358D6845594F94dc1DB02A252b5b4814aD';
    let hijackedArbAave = false;
    try {
      await network.provider.request({
        method: 'hardhat_impersonateAccount',
        params: [AAVE_POOL_ARB],
      });
      await owner.sendTransaction({ to: AAVE_POOL_ARB, value: ethers.parseEther('1.0') });
      const aaveArbSigner = await ethers.getSigner(AAVE_POOL_ARB);

      await (arbContract.connect(aaveArbSigner) as any).executeOperation(
        WETH_ARB,
        ethers.parseEther('1.0'),
        ethers.parseEther('0.0005'),
        attacker.address, // Initiator malicioso
        '0x'
      );
      hijackedArbAave = true;
    } catch (err: any) {
      hijackedArbAave = false;
      assert(
        err.message.toLowerCase().includes('revert'),
        'Guardia de Initiator bloqueó callback malicioso de Aave en Arbitrum',
        `Reversión confirmada: ${err.message.slice(0, 80)}...`
      );
    }
    assert(!hijackedArbAave, 'Atacante NO puede forzar executeOperation en Arbitrum');

  } catch (arbErr: any) {
    console.error('⚠️ Error en Fork de Arbitrum:', arbErr.message);
  }

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // RESUMEN
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  console.log('\n═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════');

  console.log('═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n');

  if (passed !== total) {
    process.exit(1);
  }

import { ethers } from "hardhat";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";

async function main() {
  console.log("🚀 Iniciando despliegue de BaseMultiDexArbitrage en Base Mainnet (ChainID 8453)...");

  const [deployer] = await ethers.getSigners();
  const balance = await ethers.provider.getBalance(deployer.address);
  console.log("👤 Deployer:", deployer.address);
  console.log("💰 Saldo ETH Nativo para Gas:", ethers.formatEther(balance), "ETH");

  if (balance === 0n) {
    throw new Error("❌ Saldo insuficiente de ETH nativo en Base para pagar el gas de despliegue.");
  }

  // 1. Desplegar Contrato
  console.log("📦 Compilando y transmitiendo transacción de despliegue...");
  const ArbitrageFactory = await ethers.getContractFactory("BaseUniversalArbitrage");
  const contract = await ArbitrageFactory.deploy();
  await contract.waitForDeployment();

  const contractAddress = await contract.getAddress();
  console.log("🎉 ¡CONTRATO DESPLEGADO EXITOSAMENTE!");
  console.log("📍 Dirección del Contrato:", contractAddress);
  console.log("🔗 Ver en BaseScan: https://basescan.org/address/" + contractAddress);

  // 2. Pre-aprobar los tokens principales para ahorrar gas en las ejecuciones
  const TOKENS_TO_APPROVE = [
    "0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006", // WETH
    "0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913", // USDC
    "0xd9aAEc86B65D86f6A7B5B1b0c42FFA531710b6CA", // USDbC
    "0x940181a94A35A4569E4529A3CDfB74e38FD98631", // AERO
    "0xcbB7C0000aB88B473b1f5aFd9ef808440eed33Bf", // cbBTC
    "0x4ed4E862860beD51a9570b96d89aF5E1B0Efefed", // DEGEN
    "0x0b3e328455c4059EEb9e3f84b5543F74E24e7E1b", // VIRTUAL
    "0x532f27101965dd16442E59d40670FaF5eBB142E4", // BRETT
    "0xAC1Bd2486aAf3B5C0fc3Fd868558b082a531B2B4", // TOSHI
  ];

  console.log("⚡ Ejecutando approveRouters() para los 9 tokens clave...");
  const txApprove = await contract.approveRouters(TOKENS_TO_APPROVE);
  await txApprove.wait(1);
  console.log("✅ Routers Uniswap V3 y Aerodrome Slipstream pre-aprobados con éxito.");

  // 3. Guardar dirección en el archivo .env
  const envPath = path.join(process.cwd(), ".env");
  if (fs.existsSync(envPath)) {
    let envContent = fs.readFileSync(envPath, "utf8");
    if (envContent.includes("ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=")) {
      envContent = envContent.replace(/ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=.*/, `ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=${contractAddress}`);
    } else {
      envContent += `\nARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE=${contractAddress}\n`;
    }
    fs.writeFileSync(envPath, envContent, "utf8");
    console.log("💾 Dirección guardada en .env como ARBITRAGE_CONTRACT_BASE");
  }
}

main().catch((error) => {
  console.error("❌ Error en despliegue:", error);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});

async function main() {

  }

  // Guardar en .env
  const fs = require("fs");
  const path = require("path");
  const envPath = path.join(process.cwd(), ".env");
